		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = UKR_authority_more_60
				check_variable = { ROOT.subjectavtoritet > 59 }
			}
		}

		allowed = { NOT = { original_tag = UKR } }

		visible = {
			ROOT = {
				OR = {
					has_autonomy_state = autonomy_republic_ukr

		}

		complete_effect = {

			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision UKR_reorganize_the_national_guard_first_brigade"

			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision remove UKR_reorganize_the_national_guard_first_brigade"
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = { ROOT = { has_full_control_of_state = PREV } }
				prioritize = { 698 }

					division = "name = \"1st Operational Brigade of the National Guard of Ukraine\" division_template = \"Natsionalna Hvardiya\" start_experience_factor = 0.1"

				UKR_verkhovna_refresh_decision_counts = yes
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }

		}

	}

	UKR_bureau_close = {
		icon = GFX_decision_ISR_centralisation_button

	# Promote Russian Interests
	UKR_promote_russian_interests = {
		icon = GFX_decision_recogn_russia_button
		cost = 50

		visible = {
			UKR_verkhovna_show_foreign_finance_decisions = yes
			UKR_promote_russian_interests_shown = yes
		}

		days_remove = 30
		days_re_enable = @ukr_verkhovna_foreign_influence_cooldown_days

		available = { UKR_promote_russian_interests_available = yes }

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision UKR_promote_russian_interests"
			hidden_effect = { UKR_verkhovna_refresh_decision_counts = yes }
		}

		remove_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision UKR_promote_russian_interests"
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = communism
				popularity = 0.03
			}
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 3 }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = SOV }
			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = UKR }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						UKR = { check_variable = { influence_array^0 = USA } }
						UKR = { check_variable = { influence_array^1 = USA } }
						UKR = { check_variable = { influence_array^2 = USA } }
						UKR = { check_variable = { influence_array^3 = USA } }
						UKR = { check_variable = { influence_array^4 = USA } }
						UKR = { check_variable = { influence_array^5 = USA } }
						UKR = { check_variable = { influence_array^6 = USA } }
					}
				}
				newline = yes
				set_temp_variable = { percent_change = -2 }
				set_temp_variable = { tag_index = USA }
				set_temp_variable = { influence_target = UKR }
				change_influence_percentage = yes

	UKR_sevastopol_naval_base_terminate_bsf_lease = {
		icon = generic_naval
		fire_only_once = yes
		custom_cost_trigger = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { political_power < UKR_bsf_lease_pp_cost } }
			NOT = { check_variable = { command_power < UKR_bsf_lease_cp_cost } }
		}
		custom_cost_text = UKR_bsf_lease_cost_text
		ai_hint_pp_cost = 750

		visible = { date < 2010.1.1 }

		available = {
			NOT = { UKR_is_pro_east = yes }
			owns_state = 669
			NOT = { is_subject_of = SOV }
			NOT = { is_in_faction_with = SOV }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = UKR_sevastopol_demand_sent }
		}

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision UKR_sevastopol_naval_base_terminate_bsf_lease"

			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { pp_change = UKR_bsf_lease_pp_cost }
				multiply_temp_variable = { pp_change = -1 }
				add_political_power = pp_change

				set_temp_variable = { cp_change = UKR_bsf_lease_cp_cost }
				multiply_temp_variable = { cp_change = -1 }
				add_command_power = cp_change

				# Cover the 3-day dispatch and the default 13-day event response window.
				set_country_flag = { flag = UKR_sevastopol_early_demand_pending_flag days = 17 value = 1 }
				SOV = { country_event = { id = ukraine_sevastopol.1 days = 3 } }
			}

			custom_effect_tooltip = TT_IF_THEY_ACCEPT

			effect_tooltip = {
				transfer_state = 1112
				SOV = {
					add_opinion_modifier = {
						target = UKR
						modifier = recent_actions_negative
					}
				}
			}

			custom_effect_tooltip = TT_IF_THEY_REJECT

			effect_tooltip = {
				add_opinion_modifier = {
					target = SOV
					modifier = recent_actions_negative
				}

				SOV = {
					add_opinion_modifier = {
						target = UKR
						modifier = recent_actions_negative
					}

					declare_war_on = {
						target = UKR
						type = take_state_focus
						generator = { 669 }
					}
				}
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 5
			modifier = { factor = 0 is_historical_focus_on = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_war = yes }
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				has_political_power > 500
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				ai_has_high_deficit = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse
			}
		}
	}

				has_country_flag = UKR_mrls_stage3_kyiv_flag

		}

			highlight_states_trigger = { state = 696 }
			highlight_color_while_active = 3
			highlight_color_before_active = 2
		}

		on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
		days_remove = 90

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_tu16_scrap"

			# 10 million plus up to 10 million more, depending on how many are still stored
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 0 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { treasury_change = UKR_storage_tu16_total }
			divide_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 30 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 1 }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.01 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes

			hidden_effect = {
				set_variable = { ukraine_has_tu16k_stockpile = 0 }
				set_variable = { ukraine_has_tu16r_stockpile = 0 }
				UKR_storage_refresh = yes
			}
		}

		remove_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision remove ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_tu16_scrap"

			set_country_flag = ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_tu16_scrap_done_flag
			custom_effect_tooltip = ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_tu16_scrapped_tt
			if = {
				limit = { owns_state = 696 controls_state = 696 }
				696 = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = UKR_scrapped_tu16_resources days = 365 } }
			}
		}
	}

				check_variable = { UKR_storage_selected_row = 52 }
			}
			ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_tu22_scrap_shown = yes
		}

		available = {
			ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_tu22_scrap_ready = yes
		}

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_states_trigger = { state = 696 }
			highlight_color_while_active = 3
			highlight_color_before_active = 2
		}

		on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
		days_remove = 90

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_tu22_scrap"

			# 10 million plus up to 10 million more, depending on how many are still stored
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 0 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { treasury_change = UKR_storage_tu22_total }
			divide_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 60 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 1 }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.01 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes

			hidden_effect = {
				set_variable = { ukraine_has_tu22kd_stockpile = 0 }
				set_variable = { ukraine_has_tu22rd_stockpile = 0 }
				UKR_storage_refresh = yes
			}
		}

		remove_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision remove ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_tu22_scrap"

			set_country_flag = ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_tu22_scrap_done_flag
			custom_effect_tooltip = ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_tu22_scrapped_tt
			if = {
				limit = { owns_state = 696 controls_state = 696 }
				696 = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = UKR_scrapped_tu22_resources days = 365 } }
			}
		}
	}

				set_variable = { ukraine_mig27_restoration_batch = 0 }
			}
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = { flag = ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_mig27_restoration_cooldown_flag days = 30 value = 1 } }
		}
	}

			highlight_color_before_active = 2
		}

		days_remove = 60

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision UKR_modernize_s125_spaa"
			custom_effect_tooltip = UKR_storage_s125_modernized_tt
			hidden_effect = { UKR_storage_s125_mark_serviced = yes }
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = UKR_storage_s125_development_cost }
				multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = UKR_modernize_s125_spaa_taken_flag }

			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision remove UKR_modernize_s125_spaa"
			UKR_count_sov_modernization = yes
			army_experience = 4
			set_country_flag = UKR_s125_pechora_2d_flag

				limit = { has_dlc = "No Step Back" }
				create_equipment_variant = {
					name = "S-125 Pechora-2D"
					type = medium_tank_aa_chassis_0
					parent_version = 0
					modules = {
						main_armament_slot = missile_launcher_system
						reload_type_slot = two_missile_pack
						turret_type_slot = tank_no_turret

						engine_type_slot = tank_diesel_engine_gen1
						secondary_armament_slot = empty
						special_type_slot_1 = tank_aux_engine
						special_type_slot_2 = empty
						special_type_slot_3 = empty
						special_type_slot_4 = spaa_off_vehicle_radar_gen1
					}
					upgrades = { tank_nsb_fire_upgrade = 4 }
					obsolete = yes
					icon = "GFX_SOV_MISSILE_1965_SAM"
				}
			}
			else = {

					name = "S-125 Pechora-2D"
					type = SP_Anti_Air_0
					allow_without_tech = yes
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = UKR_s125_form_chosen_flag } }
				country_event = ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_storage_sale.23
			}

				name = UKR_modernize_s125_spaa
				bonus = 0.25
				uses = 1
				technology = nsb_AA_upgrade_2
			}
		}

		}

			ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_brdm1_scrap_ready = yes
		}

			highlight_states_trigger = { state = 696 }
			highlight_color_while_active = 3
			highlight_color_before_active = 2
		}

		on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
		days_remove = 90

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_brdm1_scrap"

			# 10 million plus up to 10 million more, depending on how many are still stored
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = ukraine_has_brdm1_stockpile }
			divide_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 120 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 1 }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.01 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes

			hidden_effect = {
				set_variable = { ukraine_has_brdm1_stockpile = 0 }
				UKR_storage_refresh = yes
			}
		}

		remove_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision remove ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_brdm1_scrap"

			set_country_flag = ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_brdm1_scrap_done_flag
			custom_effect_tooltip = ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_brdm1_scrapped_tt
			if = {
				limit = { owns_state = 696 controls_state = 696 }
				696 = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = UKR_scrapped_brdm1_resources days = 365 } }
			}
		}
	}

			}
		}

			set_country_flag = ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_gtmu_tractors_done_flag
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = UKR_gtmu_tractors days = 3650 }
		}
	}

	ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_gtmu_scrap = {
		icon = GFX_decision_icon_UKR_modernize_sov_brdm2
		fire_only_once = yes
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 10
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
		}

		visible = {
			OR = {
				is_ai = yes
				check_variable = { UKR_storage_selected_row = 2 }
				AND = {
					check_variable = { UKR_storage_selected_row < 11 }
					has_decision = ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_gtmu_scrap
				}
				check_variable = { UKR_storage_selected_row = 22 }
			}
			ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_gtmu_scrap_shown = yes
		}

		available = {
			ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_gtmu_scrap_ready = yes
		}

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_states_trigger = { state = 696 }
			highlight_color_while_active = 3
			highlight_color_before_active = 2
		}

		on_map_mode = map_and_decisions_view
		days_remove = 90

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_gtmu_scrap"

			# 10 million plus up to 10 million more, depending on how many are still stored
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = ukraine_has_gtmu_stockpile }
			divide_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 80 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 1 }
			multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.01 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes

			hidden_effect = {
				set_variable = { ukraine_has_gtmu_stockpile = 0 }
				UKR_storage_refresh = yes
			}
		}

		remove_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision remove ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_gtmu_scrap"

			set_country_flag = ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_gtmu_scrap_done_flag
			custom_effect_tooltip = ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_gtmu_scrapped_tt
			if = {
				limit = { owns_state = 696 controls_state = 696 }
				696 = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = UKR_scrapped_gtmu_resources days = 365 } }
			}
		}
	}

				IND = {
					save_event_target_as = ukr_storage_buyer

			hidden_effect = {
				IND = { save_event_target_as = ukr_storage_buyer }
				set_temp_variable = { ukr_sale_item = 1 }
				UKR_storage_sale_close = yes
				UKR_storage_sale_failed = yes
			}
		}
	}

	# --- Help decisions while a sale mission is running: raise the buyer's influence and opinion of us ---

	UKR_storage_help_bribe = {
		icon = GFX_decision_icon_UKR_modernize_sov_btr60
		targets = { AGL ALG ARM AZE BOS CHA CHI CRO EGY ETH GEO HUN IND KAZ KYR LBA MOZ POL ROM SUD TRK VIE YEM }
		target_trigger = { FROM = { check_variable = { UKR_storage_sale_talks^num > 0 } } }

		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = UKR_storage_help_cost_50_tt
				check_variable = { treasury > 0.049 }
			}
		}

		days_remove = 30
		fixed_random_seed = no

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 5
			modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { NOT = { UKR_storage_sale_lacks_influence = yes } } } # every running sale to them has the influence it needs
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { UKR_storage_help_running > 0 } } # one bribe or meeting at a time across all buyers
			modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { treasury < 0.5 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
		}

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision UKR_storage_help_bribe target: [From.GetName]"
			custom_effect_tooltip = UKR_storage_help_bribe_tt
			hidden_effect = { add_to_variable = { UKR_storage_help_running = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.05 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}

		remove_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision UKR_storage_help_bribe resolved, target: [From.GetName]"
			hidden_effect = {
				subtract_from_variable = { UKR_storage_help_running = 1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = UKR_storage_help_running min = 0 }
			}
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 1 }
					set_temp_variable = { influence_target = FROM.id }
					change_influence_percentage = yes
				}
				30 = {
					log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: UKR_storage_help_bribe nothing happened"
				}
				20 = {
					FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = UKR_storage_opinion_bribe_exposed } }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	UKR_storage_help_meeting = {
		icon = GFX_decision_icon_UKR_modernize_sov_btr60
		targets = { AGL ALG ARM AZE BOS CHA CHI CRO EGY ETH GEO HUN IND KAZ KYR LBA MOZ POL ROM SUD TRK USA VIE YEM }
		target_trigger = { FROM = { check_variable = { UKR_storage_sale_talks^num > 0 } } }

		visible = {
			OR = {
				is_ai = yes # the AI has no other steady source of influence for these sales
				NOT = { has_country_flag = UKR_storage_meeting_done_@FROM }
			}
		}

		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = UKR_storage_help_cost_20_tt
				check_variable = { treasury > 0.019 }
			}
		}

		days_remove = 60

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 5
			modifier = { factor = 0 FROM = { UKR_storage_influence_5 = yes has_opinion = { target = ROOT value > 9 } } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { UKR_storage_help_running > 0 } } # one bribe or meeting at a time across all buyers
			modifier = { factor = 0 check_variable = { treasury < 0.5 } }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
		}

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision UKR_storage_help_meeting target: [From.GetName]"
			custom_effect_tooltip = UKR_storage_help_meeting_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = UKR_storage_meeting_done_@FROM
				add_to_variable = { UKR_storage_help_running = 1 }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.02 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}

		remove_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision UKR_storage_help_meeting finished, target: [From.GetName]"
			hidden_effect = {
				subtract_from_variable = { UKR_storage_help_running = 1 }
				clamp_variable = { var = UKR_storage_help_running min = 0 }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = FROM.id }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = FROM modifier = UKR_storage_opinion_meeting }
			FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = UKR_storage_opinion_meeting } }
		}
	}

	UKR_storage_help_deal = {
		icon = GFX_decision_icon_UKR_modernize_sov_btr60
		targets = { AGL ALG ARM AZE BOS CHA CHI CRO EGY ETH GEO HUN IND KAZ KYR LBA MOZ POL ROM SUD TRK USA VIE YEM }
		target_trigger = { FROM = { check_variable = { UKR_storage_sale_talks^num > 0 } } }

		visible = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = UKR_storage_deal_done_@FROM }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = UKR_storage_deal_pending_@FROM }
		}

		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = UKR_storage_help_cost_50_tt
				check_variable = { treasury > 0.049 }
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = UKR_storage_help_buyer_cost_tt
				FROM = { check_variable = { treasury > 0.049 } }
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = UKR_storage_help_deal_declined_tt
				FROM = {
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = UKR_storage_deal_declined }
						has_country_flag = { flag = UKR_storage_deal_declined days > 179 }
					}
				}
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 5
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai_has_high_deficit = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
		}

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision UKR_storage_help_deal target: [From.GetName]"
			custom_effect_tooltip = UKR_storage_help_deal_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = UKR_storage_deal_pending_@FROM
				FROM = {
					clr_country_flag = UKR_storage_deal_declined
					clear_array = UKR_storage_deal_sales
					for_each_loop = {
						array = UKR_storage_sale_talks
						value = ukr_deal_sale_item
						add_to_array = { UKR_storage_deal_sales = ukr_deal_sale_item }
					}
					country_event = ukr_modernize_sov_equipment_storage_sale.41
				}
			}
		}
	}

			}
		}

			set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.04 }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.04 }
			change_relative_party_popularity = yes
		}

		ai_will_do = { base = 100 }
	}
